Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [228] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度受限信念更新（selective credibility-limited belief update）框架，将复合认知输入根据每个源世界转换为更弱的代理，再进行可信度受限的转换，从而弥补现有方法将认知输入视为不可分割整体的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Katsuno-Mendelzon更新假设认知输入可以从每个初始可能世界被合并，而可信度受限信念更新虽然限制了每个源世界的后继世界，但将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表示复合输入中仅部分可实现的情形。

Method: 提出选择性可信度受限信念更新：针对每个源世界，将认知输入先转换为更弱的代理（proxy），再执行可信度受限的转换。提供对应的语义特征和公理化刻画，并识别两个良性子类：一致性保持更新算子（要求原始输入一致时每个转换后的输入必须可信）和最大一致性保持更新算子（额外要求代理在原始输入的可信后果中最大化信息量）。

Result: 可信度受限信念更新可作为该框架的特例恢复，Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新在移除可信度限制且变换函数为恒等函数时出现。证明了该框架的通用性和更强的表达能力，统一了既有方法并支持源依赖的选择性接受。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了统一且更具表达力的信念更新方法，涵盖现有方法同时支持源依赖的选择性接受。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [229] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MANTA是一个多智能体网络拓扑自适应框架，允许通信结构在推理时自我演化。通过任务条件化拓扑初始化与有界结构更新，该框架在五个基准上取得平均74.0分，超越最强基线5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理时根据实际协作情况动态调整通信结构的能力，限制了系统的适应性和性能上限。

Method: MANTA框架包含两个阶段：(1) 执行前，从先前的结构经验中初始化一个任务条件化拓扑；(2) 部署期间，监控协作痕迹，并在当前组织变得不足时应用有界结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在涵盖信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理的五个基准上，MANTA取得了最高平均得分74.0，超越最强基线5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上获得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进可以扩展到协作架构本身，即多智能体系统的通信拓扑能够动态自演化以提升任务性能，这说明架构层面的自适应是提升多智能体系统能力的重要方向。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [230] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）在资源受限的本地计算机使用智能体（CUA）中的效果，涵盖上下文、时间、结构和并行四个维度，在OSWorld基准上评估了多个本地模型。研究发现额外计算往往带来边际收益递减并改变失败模式，指出高效的本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、失败感知控制机制以及针对本地模型能力设计的智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署计算机使用智能体（CUA）对隐私、成本效率和实用性日益重要，但在严格硬件约束下提升其性能仍具挑战。虽然推理时扩展能改善前沿CUA，但其对资源受限本地模型的效果尚未被充分理解，因此需要系统性研究。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B等本地模型进行系统性实证研究，从上下文（contextual）、时间（temporal）、结构（structural）和并行（parallel）四个维度评估推理时扩展的效果。

Result: 额外计算通常带来收益递减并改变失败模式：上下文扩展提供历史依据以改善轨迹稳定性和任务准确率，但收益随token成本增加而饱和，失败从重复/停滞轨迹转向过早的虚假成功；时间扩展减少最大步数停滞但未显著提升任务成功率；结构分解给本地两阶段智能体引入规划和格式化开销；并行扩展只能部分缓解这些失败且计算成本高昂。

Conclusion: 高效的本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、失败感知控制机制以及围绕本地模型的性能与局限来设计智能体框架，而非简单地堆叠计算资源。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [231] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualG-MRAG框架，通过双层级解耦架构（宏观推理图+微观匹配图）解决多模态RAG多跳推理中粗粒度与细粒度视觉特征之间的权衡问题，利用GNN检索器和动态规划解码机制提升证据召回与复杂QA准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-RAG方法在处理复杂多跳推理任务时存在不足：实例级匹配难以捕捉跨模态、跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法面临两难困境——细粒度视觉特征导致图膨胀和检索噪声，而粗粒度表示又会丢失关键局部证据。

Method: DualG-MRAG采用双层级解耦架构：构建Macro Graph用于全局拓扑路由，Micro Graph用于细粒度局部验证，以隔离全局结构推理与局部证据匹配从而抑制检索噪声；将检索建模为查询驱动的GNN消息传递过程；通过动态规划解码机制从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代孤立文档块的输入。

Result: 大量实验表明DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂QA准确率上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦宏观推理与微观匹配的双图架构，有效解决了多模态RAG中图规模膨胀与证据丢失的矛盾，为多模态多跳推理提供了一种新的结构化检索范式。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


### [232] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun,Kanzhi Cheng,Yian Wang,Bowen Yang,Hang Yan,Liheng Chen,Fangzhi Xu,Zichen Ding,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Xingdong Gong,Zehao Li,Kaiming Jin,Xinfeng Yuan,Zhoumianze Liu,Jingyang Gong,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahui Gao,Zhiyong Wu,Tianbao Xie,Jianbing Zhang,Ben Kao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OSReward基准，系统评估VLM作为电脑使用代理(CUA)轨迹评判器的可靠性，发现现有SOTA模型存在系统性宽容偏差，并训练了OS-Shepherd开源奖励模型以较低成本实现可靠评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA轨迹验证是评估、数据整理和强化学习的核心，但人工标注无法规模化，领域转向使用VLM作为评判器。然而这些VLM评判器是否可靠这一根本问题长期未被系统考察。

Method: 构建OSReward基准：从多种代理主干的真实轨迹出发，经多阶段人工标注获得ground-truth判定；衍生OSReward-Hard挑战集和OSReward-Multi细粒度评分集；构建OS-Shepherd-100K推理标注语料，训练OS-Shepherd(9B/35B)开源奖励模型。

Result: 现有SOTA模型均未达到理想法官水平，存在系统性宽容偏差（将失败运行误判为成功）；可靠模型成本过高，低成本开源模型表现滞后；OS-Shepherd以比商业模型低30-60%的成本达到相当评判性能。

Conclusion: VLM评判器在大规模CUA轨迹验证中尚不可靠，但通过引入推理标注语料训练的轻量开源奖励模型，可以在降低成本的同时获得稳定可靠的奖励信号，为CUA社区的规模化奖励设计提供方案。

Abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

</details>


### [233] [AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28617)
*Xiangning Lin,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Haoqian Zhang,Yipeng Zhao,Chengxuan Qian,Tianwei Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Zhenlong Yuan,Dingcheng Wang,Juncheng Wu,Yuan Si,Jiaxin Liu,Baolong Bi,Robert Mahari,Tobin South,Dazza Greenwood,Zexue He,Rishi Bommasani,Sophia Kazinnik,Andreas Haupt,Samuele Marro,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了人工智能系统提示词保证（AISPA）框架，用于系统审计商业AI产品中的系统提示词，并对88个商业AI产品中的3,249条指令进行了审计，揭示了系统提示词设计差异大、保护性指令普及但覆盖浅、提示词日趋增长但问题指令仍然普遍存在的四大核心发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词由开发者配置以控制基础模型行为，在商业AI产品中广泛使用但极少向公众或监管机构披露，造成了严重的信任与问责缺口。本文旨在通过系统审计填补这一空白。

Method: 提出AISPA框架，从用户关心的八个维度系统审计系统提示词中的特定部分；随后将此框架应用于88个商业AI产品中的3,249条系统提示词指令，将每条指令分类为保护性（有利用户）或问题性（不利用户）。

Result: 四大核心发现：（1）不同产品间的系统提示词设计差异巨大；（2）98.9%的产品包含至少一条保护性指令，但仅24%覆盖全部八个维度；（3）系统提示词随时间增长且更具保护性；（4）约40%的产品包含至少一条不利于用户的指令，且保护性与问题性指令常共存于同一提示词中。

Conclusion: 研究强调了商业AI产品中系统提示词需要更高的透明度、标准化和独立监督。

Abstract: System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络的组合可靠性问题，即在多个源数据池化后重新拟合模型时，是否仍能保持各源支持的偏好排序（CBDT组合公理）。作者证明了最小二乘输出头的池化重拟合会因逆Gram几何的重新计算而反转共享偏好，提出了尺度不变的Gram失配度量、面向几何的正则化目标以及三阶段审计框架，并在负荷竞价、医学和金融决策代理场景中验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络常跨群体、时间段与运行条件合并数据以提升泛化能力，但由此带来一个可靠性质疑：在合并数据上重新拟合的模型能否保持两个数据源共同支持的行动排序？CBDT的组合公理形式化了这一要求，但现有研究并未回答固定表示神经网络何时满足该性质，本文旨在将此组合可靠性从定性规范转变为可度量和可操作的工具。

Method: (1) 理论分析：证明池化重拟合的本质是重新计算用于加权源证据的逆Gram几何，可导致共享偏好反转，并推导出精确和近似的保持条件；(2) 提出尺度不变的Gram失配度量用于候选池优先级排序，以及面向几何的正则化以在训练中塑造源几何；(3) 开发三阶段审计流程，将严格成对偏好反转追溯到任务定义的效用损失。

Result: 在负荷竞价、医学和金融决策代理实验中发现存在稳定池化与易反转池化两种区域：负荷审计识别出在代理效用下可测的非零类源共识-相对有害决策；跨域审计显示可比的失配程度可能对应截然不同的保持率。面向几何的目标在描述准确性、一致性、几何与损害之间占据不同的权衡操作点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛选、解析认证、面向几何的训练和决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可度量且可操作，为跨源数据池化的神经网络模型提供了系统的可靠性质检工具。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [235] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了进化动态优化中时间鲁棒性（ROOT）范式下固定部署解的期望生存时间，针对各向同性高斯环境动力学建立了离散首次退出问题的分析框架，推导了严格的生存时间下界和可计算的多步上界，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROOT领域虽已发展成熟，但绝大多数贡献是算法和经验的，缺乏理论基础。生存时间作为衡量时间鲁棒性的关键指标，其期望值如何依赖于环境动态、部署质量和问题特性尚不清楚。理解这一性质对于在实际中做出部署决策至关重要。

Method: 将生存时间建模为离散首次退出问题，在固定部署解和各向同性高斯环境动力学假设下，推导期望生存时间的严格下界和可计算的多步上界，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行验证，同时检验对建模假设和参数不确定性的敏感性。

Result: 分析表明，在缓慢变化的环境中期望生存时间以Θ(σ^-2)的规模增长，并在高维场景下趋近其最小值（即仅存活一个环境变化周期）。蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测，并展示了边界如何支持优化后的部署决策。

Conclusion: 该框架为部署寿命提供了分析性刻画，能够识别所需部署时间跨度是否可以保证、被排除或仍无法从分析上确定，从而为ROOT领域提供了重要的理论基础和实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [236] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上等距回归（isotonic regression）的最坏情况自由度给出了完全锐利的有限样本刻画，确定了使其拟合值数量最大化的二元序列。作者利用解析数论得到了自由度的锐利上界，其主项系数为 3/(4π²)^(1/3) n^(2/3)，优于此前的结果。基于此自由度界，进一步推导出等距回归期望校准误差（ECE）的第一个非平凡的分布无关保证，该保证完全模型无关、分布无关，仅假设 Y ∈ {0,1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等距回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但其自由度（即有效参数数量）的最坏情况刻画此前并不完全锐利，且其在校准误差上的分布无关保证此前缺乏非平凡结果。作者旨在填补这两个理论空白。

Method: (1) 通过解析数论方法（涉及 ζ 函数、Dirichlet 级数等工具相关技术）对二元序列中等距回归拟合值数量进行精确计数，得到最坏情况下的锐利有限样本刻画；(2) 计算自由度上界，主项为 3/(4π²)^(1/3) n^(2/3)；(3) 将该确定性自由度界嵌入校准分析框架，推导出仅依赖二元标签假设的 ECE 分布无关上界。

Result: 获得自由度最坏情形的精确特征：识别出最大化拟合值数量的二元序列，并证明自由度上界主项为 3/(4π²)^(1/3) n^(2/3)，显著改进此前界。同时，首次推导出等距回归 ECE 的非平凡、完全分布无关的上界，仅假设 Y ∈ {0,1}。

Conclusion: 本文在理论和应用两个层面取得进展：理论上完全解决了二元样本上等距回归最坏情形自由度问题，应用上提供了等距回归校准误差的第一个非平凡分布无关保证，为概率预测校准的理论分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [237] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（基于均值的Huber聚合），一种通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值的稳健估计方法，它在中位数式稳健性和经验均值效率之间插值，并建立了Hilbert空间中的多数定理和MOM阶偏差界，在有限二阶矩下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布会削弱经验均值的高置信控制，而几何中位数均值法（MOM）缺乏一个能向均值效率靠近的阈值参数，导致其在小样本条件下效率不足。作者希望设计一种方法，能够同时兼具稳健性和接近经验均值的统计效率。

Method: HOMER通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值，其规范形式（canonical form）和伪Huber形式（pseudo-Huber form）都能限制每个块的得分，并在中位数式稳健性与经验均值之间实现插值。规范HOMER在其二次区域内可恢复样本均值，伪HOMER随阈值增大趋近样本均值，二者还支持渐近线性性和一致的sandwich协方差估计。

Result: 在有限三阶矩下，固定有限维投影支持以通常参数速率进行均值推断，需要块大小和块数同时增长且块大小增长更快。重尾模拟表明HOMER在少数块摘要被位移时保持稳定；在干净高斯数据上两种版本都接近经验均值的效率；有限块sandwich区间在偏态函数数据上覆盖不足；当污染影响大多数块或干扰普通块内均值时方法失效。

Conclusion: HOMER作为块均值聚合方法，在理论上获得了Hilbert空间多数定理和MOM阶偏差界的支撑，能够在稳健性与效率间灵活权衡。但其在有限块下的推断覆盖、重度污染场景的性能以及依赖块内均值完整性等方面仍有局限，需要进一步的改进和应用研究。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [238] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的组合优化方法，用于近红外光谱中糖含量估计的波长区域选择。该方法构建稀疏二次代理模型，通过汤普森采样提取感兴趣波长区域，并将采集函数最小化转化为二次无约束二元优化问题，采用模拟退火或量子退火求解。实验证明该方法优于基于遗传算法和模拟退火的选择方法，且在局部扰动下具有稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波长选择是近红外光谱分析中重要的预处理手段，旨在提升预测精度和模型可解释性。传统的波长选择方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）存在收敛不稳定、易过拟合等问题，因此需要一种更鲁棒的波长区域选择方法。

Method: 将波长区域选择建模为二元黑盒优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化框架：构建稀疏二次代理模型逼近目标函数，通过汤普森采样顺序提取感兴趣的波长区域，并将采集函数最小化转化为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，通过模拟退火或量子退火求解。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在偏最小二乘回归上的预测精度优于遗传算法和模拟退火方法，选出的波长区域更加一致。在一比特局部扰动下，验证集均方根误差波动极小，说明方法收敛到更平滑的误差曲面，避免了孤立的过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化是光谱预测任务中鲁棒特征选择的有效框架，能够提供稳定且可解释的波长区域选择结果。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [239] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于深度神经网络的Engression方法进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑性结构下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression通过能量评分拟合生成模型来学习条件分布，但缺乏严格的理论误差分析，尤其是使用深度神经网络实现时的收敛性质尚不明确。

Method: 将Engression的超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三部分，并在此分解基础上，假设目标条件生成器具有组合光滑性结构，推导收敛速率。

Result: 建立了在组合光滑性假设下Engression（深度神经网络实现）的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该理论分析为Engression方法的统计性质提供了严格保证，明确了其误差来源及收敛行为。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [240] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RPC（随机投影与裁剪）方法解决多物品用户在本地差分隐私（LDP）场景下的投毒攻击防御问题，通过随机投影和精确偏差校正，在不牺牲偏差-方差权衡的前提下收缩输出空间，显著增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP协议易受投毒攻击，已有防御策略仅针对单物品用户，而多物品用户因输出空间更大，攻击者能实施更强的攻击而不被发现，防御更具挑战性。

Method: RPC方法包括两个核心步骤：(1) 服务器向每个用户发送随机二进制向量，用户将本地数据投影到该向量上，并对投影值进行裁剪以限制攻击者的能力；(2) 针对裁剪引入的偏差，通过精细分析推导出偏差的精确表达式并提出校正方法，从而无需再进行偏差-方差权衡，可进一步降低裁剪阈值以缩小输出空间、增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在可信环境下，RPC性能与现有方法相当或更优，说明其本身已是高效的估计器；在不可信环境下，RPC对投毒攻击的鲁棒性显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RPC通过消除偏差-方差权衡，同时实现了高估计效率和强抗投毒攻击能力，为多物品用户的LDP协议提供了可靠的鲁棒估计方案。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [241] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）框架下研究对抗训练，建立了源一致泛化误差界并给出匹配下界，揭示了对抗训练存在的统计精度损失（源于鲁棒性与观测噪声的相互作用）。提出两阶段噪声去偏程序来消除混合项中的噪声贡献，使估计器达到极小极大多项式速率（至多为对数因子），并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是防御对抗攻击的强有力方法，但其泛化性能与统计精度的理论刻画尚不充分。已有工作难以清晰解释对抗训练中鲁棒性与泛化之间的权衡关系，特别是在非参数框架下缺乏系统性的泛化误差界分析。

Method: 在RKHS框架中通过核积分算子研究对抗训练估计器。首先推导源一致泛化误差界（依赖于鲁棒水平、样本量、源平滑度和核谱），然后建立匹配下界，揭示对抗训练相对极小极大预测基准存在统计精度损失。提出两阶段噪声去偏程序：先估计噪声贡献，再从混合鲁棒项中去除噪声，从而改进泛化速率。

Result: 在多项式谱模型下，对抗训练的最优平衡泛化率可能慢于极小极大预测基准；噪声去偏后的估计器在适当选择样本依赖的鲁棒水平时，可达到极小极大多项式速率（至多差对数因子）。数值实验支持理论结论并验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 对抗训练在非参数框架中的泛化行为得到完整刻画；鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互作用是统计精度损失的根本来源。两阶段噪声去偏方法为平衡对抗鲁棒性与泛化性能提供了原理性解决方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [242] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展至非平稳非线性回归场景，可同时识别相关特征的子集和子空间。在混沌动力学（Lorenz-96）和托卡马克等离子体湍流（Hasegawa-Wakatani）等极具挑战性的问题上，EOMR以线性复杂度实现鲁棒学习，在预测精度和模型复杂度上均显著优于梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络及TabPFN等SOTA工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流机器学习和AI工具（如梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络、基于Transformer的工具）在处理非平稳和非线性回归问题，尤其是混沌和流体动力学等复杂物理系统时，预测误差和模型复杂度均表现不佳。作者旨在引入一种能够同时进行特征子集和子空间识别的聚类扩展方法，以解决这些高维小数据学习中的挑战。

Method: 将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展为熵最优流形回归（EOMR），使其能够在非平稳和非线性回归中联合、同步地识别相关特征的子集和子空间。算法具备线性扩展的迭代与内存复杂度。在三个基准上进行验证：(i) Lorenz-96系统强混沌（F=8）与极强混沌（F=12）态预测；(ii) 托卡马克边缘等离子体的Hasegawa-Wakatani模型数据。

Result: EOMR在Lorenz-96和Hasegawa-Wakatani基准上均显著优于SOTA方法：相比梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN v0.3，EOMR的均方根预测误差低数个数量级，模型复杂度也低数个数量级。对于Hasegawa-Wakatani案例，EOMR提炼出仅含8个参数的线性、因果、弱平稳自回归过程，即可简洁且技巧性地描述主导Essential Orthogonal Function（EOF）动力学。

Conclusion: EOMR作为一种新颖的回归方法，在混沌和湍流等极端复杂动力系统的预测任务中展现出卓越的精度-复杂度权衡优势，并能从数据中蒸馏出简洁可解释的物理描述（如8参数自回归模型），证明了其在挑战性高维小数据回归问题上的强大潜力。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [243] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该综述系统回顾了深度学习中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，重点聚焦于集成近似和贝叶斯近似方法及其输出的汇总度量，将方法分为五个家族，并统一区分了产生预测分布的方法与汇总其不确定性的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统架构缺乏原则性的不确定性量化机制，现有UQ综述对高效集成近似和单遍方法的深度覆盖不足，且缺乏统一的方法论框架。

Method: 将方法组织为五个家族（贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单遍方法），对每个方法考察理论动机、实现、实证表现和局限性；同时评述集成多样性理论、不确定性度量及其分解（对比熵分解与成对散度度量），并整合评价方法论，最后讨论大语言模型中的不确定性与开放研究方向。

Result: 本文提供了一份结构化的批判性综述，统一了UQ方法中"生成预测分布的方法"与"汇总不确定性的度量"两个层面，并在高效集成近似与单遍方法上提供了现有综述所欠缺的深度讨论。

Conclusion: 未来研究开放方向包括：分类任务的高效认知不确定性度量、最后一层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性与校准，以及混合架构的研究。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [244] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-FRL（Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning），一种交叉拟合框架，将不规则功能性历史数据（如实验室值、生理信号等）转化为针对特定估计目标（estimand）的状态表示，以支持双稳健估计器。理论上证明了当所选状态保留EIF所需的干扰信息时，表示误差与普通干扰误差同阶，均值估计器在显式条件下具有渐近线性。模拟实验显示在高维功能混杂、信息性测量、弱支持或重尾伪结果场景下均有增益；VitalDB实际审计表明该框架可行，且得出一个有用的阴性结论：对于该ICU处置端点，标量实验室摘要已携带大部分端点相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究常记录为不规则的功能性片段（不等间隔、信息性测量时间），但标准双稳健估计器通常需要标量摘要，而序列学习器优化的预测损失不一定会稳定有效影响函数（EIF），两者存在缺口。

Method: DR-FRL采用交叉拟合工作流：功能性和时序编码器将点云和先前历史映射为状态；干扰头估计结局、治疗和删失函数；通过EIF靶向验证、校准、重叠、尾部和消融诊断来评估状态是否支撑估计方程。Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计器单独处理，并非Wald推断的替代。

Result: 理论结果表明，若状态保留EIF所需干扰信息，表示误差进入与普通干扰误差相同的二阶乘积余项，均值估计器在显式率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下渐近线性。模拟实验显示在高维功能混杂、信息性测量、弱支持或重尾伪结果场景下有性能增益。VitalDB审计显示框架可用，但对ICU处置端点，标量实验室摘要已足够。

Conclusion: DR-FRL弥合了功能序列表示学习与双稳健推断之间的鸿沟，提供了一种将不规则历史转化为估计目标靶向状态的系统方法，并附带完整的诊断体系。该框架既在理论上保证了渐近性质，也在实践中验证了可操作性，同时揭示了在某些端点下简单标量摘要已足够这一有价值的阴性发现。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在d-单纯形上，对于相对于负熵L-光滑的凸函数类，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，并且当d=Ω(T²)时成立。该结果表明镜像下降在该函数类中是最优的（至多差一个对数因子），并进一步将此结果扩展到量子设定下的负冯·诺依曼熵情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Dragomir等人已证明在相对光滑性假设下加速可能无法实现，但其构造的prox函数是病态的、与困难实例共同构造的。本文旨在证明即使对于具有特别有利结构的特定prox函数（负熵），加速仍然不可能，从而揭示了一个令人惊讶的事实：尽管在ℓ1范数光滑性下加速方法易于获得，但在相对光滑性框架下非加速是本质性的。

Method: 本文通过构造下界证明，在凸且相对负熵L-光滑的函数类中，针对任意一阶方法（当维数d=Ω(T²)时）证明收敛率下界Ω(L/T)。关键分为两个部分：(1) 经典设定：以标准d-单纯形上的负熵为prox函数；(2) 量子设定：将结果推广到谱面体上关于负冯·诺依曼熵相对L-光滑的函数类。

Result: 证明了在所有一阶方法中，关于负熵相对光滑的凸函数最小化收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，表明镜像下降在此类中最优（至多差对数因子）；该结果同样在量子设定（负冯·诺依曼熵）下成立。

Conclusion: 即使在具有良好结构的prox函数（负熵）下，相对光滑框架中加速仍然不可实现，镜像下降的Ω(L/T)收敛率是该设定下的本质下界，填补了此前研究（仅针对病态prox函数）的空白，并将结论自然延伸到量子计算场景。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [246] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测（prognostics）的混合规划方法，用于推导预测性维护策略。该方法综合考虑决策选项、预测模型提供的系统未来状态信息以及更新-奖励过程的成本，最终生成仅由少量参数定义的策略。在预防性更换和预防性订货两个决策场景中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性维护策略往往复杂且容易过拟合，缺乏高效且稳健的规划方法。作者旨在设计一种既能利用预测信息、又能控制策略复杂度的方法，以提高维护决策的效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出混合规划方法，融合三个要素：可用决策选项、预测模型给出的系统未来状态信息、以及底层更新-奖励过程的成本。策略只需少量参数，这些参数可基于理论推导或通过运行至失效（run-to-failure）数据优化获得。

Result: 在预防性更换和预防性订货两个决策场景的数值实验中，所推导的策略与优化的基准策略性能相当，同时在效率和抗过拟合能力上显著更优。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法能以较低的策略复杂度获得接近最优的性能，并且对过拟合具有更强的鲁棒性，为预测性维护决策提供了一种实用且高效的方案。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [247] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了以最小时间函数为框架的、目标集为集值映射的广义多源Weber问题，建立了全局与局部最优解的存在性，分析了解集的定性性质（闭性、紧性、有界/无界性），推导了目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，并研究了全局与局部解映射的稳定性，从而提供了定量与定性的敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多源Weber问题通常假定目标为点集，而实际应用中目标往往是不确定的、以集值形式呈现的。作者将问题推广到集值目标框架下，并借助最小时间函数进行刻画，旨在弥补现有理论在解的存在性、解集性质以及稳定性分析方面的不足。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数作为目标函数的建模工具，综合运用变分分析与集值分析的理论框架。具体包括：（1）利用最优性条件与拓扑工具证明全局和局部解的存在性；（2）通过解集结构分析研究其闭性、紧性及有界/无界性条件；（3）借助Lipschitz常数估计推导目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的连续性；（4）将全局与局部解映射视为集值映射，研究其上半连续、下半连续等稳定性性质。

Result: 作者获得了以下主要结果：全局与局部最优解的存在性定理；解集闭性、紧性以及有界/无界性的充分条件；目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性估计；全局和局部解映射在集值分析意义下的稳定性刻画。这些结果为该问题提供了系统的定量与定性敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 本文在最小时间函数框架下对广义多源Weber问题进行了全面的理论分析，不仅建立了解的存在性与解集的结构性质，还给出了关于目标集扰动的连续性与稳定性结果，为不确定环境下多源选址问题的理论分析提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [248] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出OptGraph，首个引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化智能体工作流，通过构建可复用的类型化图知识（涵盖建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误修正），利用图邻域信息丰富检索上下文，并支持自适应知识更新。在基准数据集上平均精确准确率比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架高8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动进化优化方法存在三个局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的细化机制、跨多样任务的检索鲁棒性有限。这些限制阻碍了LLM在进化优化任务中的效果与泛化能力。

Method: OptGraph由三部分组成：(1) 将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕获建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节与错误修正之间的关系；(2) 推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索知识，为建模、验证和迭代细化提供结构化上下文；(3) 支持自适应知识更新，将执行轨迹和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的图知识，无需对LLM进行参数微调。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，OptGraph的平均精确准确率比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架高8.9%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OptGraph验证了将GraphRAG引入LLM驱动的进化优化流程的有效性，通过结构化图知识库和自适应更新机制，显著提升了自动优化框架的准确性，为后续可选的无微调知识蒸馏方向提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [249] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了由一维粘性Burgers方程驱动、含局部内控制的二次最优跟踪问题的全局指数turnpike性质。对任意初始数据，当周期跟踪目标足够小时，有限时域最优解趋近唯一最优周期态；零目标情形在无初始小性假设下得到原点处的全局稳态turnpike。这是粘性Burgers方程首批全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的粘性Burgers方程turnpike结果多为局部或半全局性质，尚未有全局指数turnpike的结论。本文旨在填补这一空白，在无初始数据小性限制下建立全局收敛到最优周期稳态（或原点）的性质。

Method: 证明方法结合两部分：(1) 通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论获得局部指数turnpike；(2) 利用抛物耗散论证给出与时间域无关的吸收时间，从而将局部结果扩展为全局性质。

Result: 对于周期跟踪目标足够小的情况，对所有初始数据，有限时域最优解指数趋近唯一最优周期态；零目标情形下，无需初始数据小性假设即可全局收敛到原点稳态周期。这些是粘性Burgers方程的首批全局指数turnpike结果。

Conclusion: 通过结合严格的凸性条件、周期Riccati理论与抛物耗散机制，成功建立了粘性Burgers方程最优控制问题的全局指数turnpike性质，所得结果在初始数据上全局成立，显著推广了此前仅限局部的结论。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [250] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维布朗运动和独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产下的投资组合效用最大化问题，允许策略包含跳跃信号，通过鞅最优性原理推导带跳跃的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE）来刻画值函数和最优策略，并证明解的存在唯一性，同时处理异质性跳跃信号的特例并给出数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一维指数效用情形下的方法推广到多维布朗运动与跳跃过程驱动的更一般设定，并考虑投资者接收跳跃信号（包括异质性跳跃信号）时的最优投资组合选择问题，以填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 首先将投资组合动态表示为半鞅过程；其次利用鞅最优性原理推导带跳跃的BSDE来刻画值函数和最优策略；随后证明带跳跃的BSDE解的存在唯一性；处理投资者接收异质性跳跃信号的特例情形；最后以高斯信号下的对数效用情形进行数值模拟。

Result: 成功推导出带跳跃的BSDE以刻画值函数和最优策略，证明了该BSDE解的存在唯一性，给出了异质性跳跃信号情形下的处理框架，并通过高斯信号的对数效用数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究将指数效用情形下基于BSDE的方法推广至包含跳跃信号的更一般多维设置，为存在跳跃风险时的最优投资组合选择提供了理论基础和数值验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [251] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种严格的数学框架，解决矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集合选择的NP-hard逆合成问题，通过证明结构不可控性仅源于维度特异性可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并设计两阶段合成方法（可达性前提+三种对称破缺算法）保证强结构可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵加权网络中保证强结构可控性(SSC)的最小领导者集合选择是一个尚未解决的NP-hard难题，现有方法无法高效处理该逆合成问题，亟需严格的数学框架来求解。

Method: 先证明结构不可控性仅由维度特异性可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价两因素导致；据此构建两阶段合成框架：第一阶段为可达性前提，识别结构根节点；第二阶段采用三种对称破缺算法（贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模边界最大化、分区熵最大化）来消除拓扑对称性。通过数学证明确保对不变子空间和结构扩张具有免疫性。

Result: 在多种拓扑结构上的大量数值评估验证了所提框架的有效性，数学证明保证了方法对不变子空间和结构膨胀因素的免疫性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次以严格数学框架系统解决了矩阵加权网络中的最小领导者集选择问题，为SSC保障提供了理论完备且算法可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [252] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限期界的劳动力规划问题，其中每期员工流失服从二项分布（参数取决于招聘后劳动力水平），并考虑固定招聘成本。通过引入"二项K凸性"证明Bellman函数下K凸性的保持性，得出最优招聘策略具有(s,S)结构，并提出分段逼近方法转化为MILP问题进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中劳动力规划问题中，员工流失率会随劳动力规模变化（决策依赖的转移概率），且招聘存在固定成本。现有研究缺乏对这类具有决策依赖二项转移概率的随机动态规划的凸性分析，需要建立相应理论框架来刻画最优策略结构。

Method: 1) 建立有限期界随机动态规划模型，流失服从决策依赖的二项分布；2) 定义"Binomial-K-convexity"概念，证明其在Bellman函数二项传播下的K凸性保持性；3) 证明最优策略具有(s,S)结构；4) 开发分段线性逼近方法，将问题转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解各期(s,S)参数。

Result: 数值实验表明所提出的方法计算速度快且最优性间隙小，验证了模型的正确性和算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为具有决策依赖二项转移概率的有限期界劳动力规划问题提供了理论支撑（K凸性保持性）与实用求解框架（MILP逼近），证明了最优策略的(s,S)结构，兼顾理论完备性与计算效率。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [253] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究测度空间中的优化问题，其中正则项包含到给定先验测度的传输距离。利用弱星二阶次微分理论推导无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，并在额外假设下证明与二次增长的等价性。进一步计算了弱星二阶次微分并证明了弱星上微分存在性，最后将结果应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测度空间中的优化问题广泛出现在最优传输、最优控制等领域，但二阶最优性条件（特别是无间隙型的必要条件与充分条件一致）在该框架下的研究尚不充分，需要发展合适的二阶变分理论。

Method: 采用弱星二阶次微分理论作为核心工具，结合Kantorovich对偶问题的最优势函数（Kantorovich势），在目标函数光滑部分和势函数满足额外假设的条件下推导二阶最优性条件的等价刻画，并证明弱星上微分性。

Result: 得到了测度空间优化问题中无间隙型二阶最优性条件与二次增长之间的等价性，计算了弱星二阶次导数的显式表达式，并证明了弱星上微分不同性，同时将该理论成功应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 弱星二阶次微分理论为带传输距离正则项的测度空间优化问题提供了完整的二阶变分分析框架，其等价性结果和可微性性质为后续算法设计和理论分析奠定了坚实基础，并可直接推广至最优控制等应用场景。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [254] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限个适当、下半连续、凸函数的近端算子方程系统的精确解存在性与唯一性，建立了近似解存在的充要条件，并引入了逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，给出了多种刻画，最后应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影问题（即一族闭凸集上的投影方程组）自然推广到近端算子方程组，但这类广义系统的解的存在性、唯一性及结构性刻画尚不完善，需要建立系统性的理论框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 利用凸分析和非光滑优化的工具，建立了近端算子方程组精确解存在的充要条件；在此基础上引入逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，并给出其多种等价刻画；最后将理论结果应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。

Result: 给出了近端算子方程组近似解存在的必要与充分条件，建立了逆近端性质（IPP）的多种刻画，并证明了该框架在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中的适用性与有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的近端算子方程组求解框架及其逆近端性质（IPP）理论，为Hilbert空间中的优化与反问题提供了统一且有力的数学工具，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [255] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图（DD）的割平面生成方法，用于求解二元多项式优化（BPO）问题。该方法针对任意选定的支持集，在多线性多胞形中分离出面定义割并提升回原问题，利用一种基于递归公式的紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量而将超边变量隐式编码在状态中。该编码还提供了多线性多胞形的扩展表述、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（揭示了新的多项式可解超图类）以及生成割面性的证明。实验表明该方法在根节点实现更大的间隙闭合且使用更少的割，显著加速分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，二元多项式优化中多线性集上的强割（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）是针对固定支持模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性和自动化手段。作者旨在提出一种系统化、可计算的方法来为任意支持模式生成面定义割，从而克服手工推导的局限性。

Method: 提出决策图（DD）方法：对于任意选定的支持集，在局部多线性多胞形中分离面定义割并提升回原问题。核心创新是紧凑DD编码，基于递归公式仅编码顶点变量，超边变量隐式嵌入状态表示。由此推导出：多线性多胞形的扩展表述、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（发现新的多项式可解超图类）、以及生成割的面性证书。此外设计了三种支持策略（复用、扩展、分区为节超图）用于割生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，DD方法在根节点实现了比现有方法更大的间隙闭合，且使用更少的割，并显著加速了分支定界过程。

Conclusion: 决策图方法为二元多项式优化提供了系统化、自动化的割平面生成框架，不仅克服了传统手工推导特定支持模式割的局限，还通过理论上界（宽度刻画）和计算性能两方面验证了其有效性，展现出在求解BPO问题上的显著优势。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>
